It is SO good- perfectly seasoned, and deliciously paired with a tangy and balanced cocktail sauced.\n\nI have had many different dishes at Sea N Suds, including but not limited to:\nseafood gumbo (see video), peel and eat steamed shrimp (see photo), fried shrimp, popcorn shrimp, fried crab claws, fried oysters, oysters on the half shell, royal reds, fried mullet, fish of the day, fried catfish, fried okra, fried cheese, fried mushrooms, hush puppies, onion rings, coleslaw, and virgin strawberry daiquiris.\n\nYou literally cannot go wrong with any of these choices- or anything on the menu for that matter. I recently also tried the piña colada on the rocks (see photo), which was sweet and delicious!\n\nEverything here is always served fresh with a smile! Some of the servers at Sea N Suds have been there since I was a young child. If that doesn’t scream positive atmosphere, I don’t know what does.\n\nDue to the popularity of this Gulf Shores staple, there is usually a wait to be seated. But don’t worry, they have plenty to keep you entertained while you wait, including beach access, a deck with stunning ocean views, a fun bar, and a lovely little gift shop. --- entity_google_reviews [row 2 of 5] ---
review_id: 1513
entity_slug: sea-n-suds
google_review_name: places/ChIJ6VNVkr4EmogRaTPu5yA61fM/reviews/Ci9DQUlRQUNvZENodHljRjlvT2xSRGMyWXpORlpmZW5VdGNHUkVaMlJVV1d4RGJtYxAB
rating: 5
review_text: Sea N Suds is one of my absolute favorite restaurants in Gulf Shores! My family and I have been coming here for over 25 years. The food and service has been consistently good for as long as I can remember. Whether you are just coming off the beach for a quick lunch or heading out for a family dinner, Sea N Suds has something for you.
I always enjoy my meal with a cup of their plentiful seafood gumbo and a glass of sweet iced tea. Leading up to our most recent trip to Gulf Shores, I was literally dreaming of their peel and eat steamed shrimp. It is SO good- perfectly seasoned, and deliciously paired with a tangy and balanced cocktail sauced.

I have had many different dishes at Sea N Suds, including but not limited to:
seafood gumbo (see video), peel and eat steamed shrimp (see photo), fried shrimp, popcorn shrimp, fried crab claws, fried oysters, oysters on the half shell, royal reds, fried mullet, fish of the day, fried catfish, fried okra, fried cheese, fried mushrooms, hush puppies, onion rings, coleslaw, and virgin strawberry daiquiris.

You literally cannot go wrong with any of these choices- or anything on the menu for that matter. I recently also tried the piña colada on the rocks (see photo), which was sweet and delicious!

Everything here is always served fresh with a smile! Some of the servers at Sea N Suds have been there since I was a young child. If that doesn’t scream positive atmosphere, I don’t know what does.

Due to the popularity of this Gulf Shores staple, there is usually a wait to be seated. But don’t worry, they have plenty to keep you entertained while you wait, including beach access, a deck with stunning ocean views, a fun bar, and a lovely little gift shop. I have never felt the dread of waiting to be seated, but rather been excited to go and watch the gulls and dolphins while sipping a fun drink prior to a fantastic meal.

Sea N Suds is a must for me every time I’m in the Gulf Shores/Orange Beach area. I highly recommend giving this wonderful restaurant a try next time you’re in town!

author_name: Sarah Buchanan

review_text: Sea-N-Suds in Gulf Shores is a casual, waterfront seafood restaurant that delivers fresh, flavorful dishes in a relaxed, friendly atmosphere. Known for its Gulf Coast favorites, the menu features fresh shrimp, oysters, fish sandwiches, and platters, all prepared with care and generous portions. Daily specials and seasonal offerings keep the menu interesting for repeat visitors, while sides like hush puppies, fries, and coleslaw complement the seafood perfectly.

The setting is informal and welcoming, with indoor and outdoor seating that lets guests enjoy the coastal breeze and view. The staff is known for being personable and attentive, helping customers navigate the menu and ensuring a smooth dining experience. Takeout is also a convenient option for those who want to enjoy seafood by the water or at home.

body: Sea N Suds is one of my absolute favorite restaurants in Gulf Shores! My family and I have been coming here for over 25 years. The food and service has been consistently good for as long as I can remember. Whether you are just coming off the beach for a quick lunch or heading out for a family dinner, Sea N Suds has something for you.
I always enjoy my meal with a cup of their plentiful seafood gumbo and a glass of sweet iced tea. Leading up to our most recent trip to Gulf Shores, I was literally dreaming of their peel and eat steamed shrimp. It is SO good- perfectly seasoned, and deliciously paired with a tangy and balanced cocktail sauced.

I have had many different dishes at Sea N Suds, including but not limited to:
seafood gumbo (see video), peel and eat steamed shrimp (see photo), fried shrimp, popcorn shrimp, fried crab claws, fried oysters, oysters on the half shell, royal reds, fried mullet, fish of the day, fried catfish, fried okra, fried cheese, fried mushrooms, hush puppies, onion rings, coleslaw, and virgin strawberry daiquiris.

You literally cannot go wrong with any of these choices- or anything on the menu for that matter. I recently also tried the piña colada on the rocks (see photo), which was sweet and delicious!

Everything here is always served fresh with a smile! Some of the servers at Sea N Suds have been there since I was a young child. If that doesn’t scream positive atmosphere, I don’t know what does.

Due to the popularity of this Gulf Shores staple, there is usually a wait to be seated. But don’t worry, they have plenty to keep you entertained while you wait, including beach access, a deck with stunning ocean views, a fun bar, and a lovely little gift shop. I have never felt the dread of waiting to be seated, but rather been excited to go and watch the gulls and dolphins while sipping a fun drink prior to a fantastic meal.

Sea N Suds is a must for me every time I’m in the Gulf Shores/Orange Beach area. I highly recommend giving this wonderful restaurant a try next time you’re in town!
